        "text": "Material: Stainless Steel; High ... length: 0.1520 inch (3.86 mm); Solid height: 0.0960 inch (2.44 mm); Spring rate: 107 lbs/in (187 N/cm);...\n\nCrest-to-Crest Wave Springs are pre-stacked in series, decreasing the spring rate proportionally to the number of turns. Uses are typically applications requiring low-medium spring rates and large deflections with low-medium forces. Among the major advantages, this design eliminates the need to keep the wave crests aligned. The need to use a key locating device, or to insert a shim between individual springs is not necessary. Because the spring is integrally formed, the wave peaks hold their configuration. As a replacement for helical compression springs, Crest-to-Crest springs can develop similar forces, yet occupy one-half (1/2) or less the axial space. This allows for strict space constraints. Crest-to-Crest Wave Springs will maintain the same force and load specifications of a conventional round wire spring, but with the advantages of resultant lowered and compacted operating heights, free heights and solid heights.\n\nMaterial: Stainless Steel; High Carbon Steel; Coil Shape: Straight; Spring Pitch: Constant Pitch; Base Unit System: English Design; Outer Diameter: 0.3120 inch (7.92 mm); Inner Diameter: 0.2000 inch (5.08 mm); Wire diameter: 0.0320 inch (0.8128 mm); Free length: 0.1520 inch (3.86 mm); Solid height: 0.0960 inch (2.44 mm); Spring rate: 107 lbs/in (187 N/cm);",

      "file_text": "# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n# =============================================================================\n# CSM V3 -- SINKER RING V1.0\n# =============================================================================\n# File:    CSM_V3_SinkerRing_V1_0.FCMacro\n# Date:    May 2026\n# Target:  C:\\3D-Project\\01_MECHANICAL\\07_KNITTING\\freecad_macros\\\n#\n# DESIGN INTENT:\n#   Stationary ring around rotating cylinder with 72 sinker projections.\n#   Sinkers hold yarn loop while needle drops to form stitch.\n#   6 feeder windows allow yarn entry without removing sinkers (tapered).\n#\n# CRITICAL DESIGN RULES:\n#   - NEVER remove sinkers at feeder zones (only taper height)\n#   - Sinkers offset by 2.5deg from needle slots (between needles)\n#   - All 72 sinkers retain function\n#   - Smoothstep tapers in feeder zones (no sharp transitions)\n#\n# DIMENSIONS (LOCKED):\n#   Ring centers\n#   72 sinkers at 5deg pitch (360/72)\n#   6 feeders at 60deg intervals (0, 60, 120, 180, 240, 300deg)\n#   Window width: ~8mm circumferential at sinker tip radius\n#\n# MOUNTING:\n#   3 dowel pins at PCD 125mm: 1 round + 2 slotted\n#   Apply Loctite 603 for retention\n# =============================================================================\n\nimport FreeCAD\nimport Part\nimport math\nimport os\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CONFIGURATION\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Output paths\nBASE = r\"C:\\3D-Project\\01_MECHANICAL\\07_KNITTING\\SinkerRing_V1_0\"\nPATH_STL_FULL  = os.path.join(BASE, \"CSM_V3_SinkerRing_V1_0_FULL.stl\")\nPATH_STL_SLICE = os.path.join(BASE, \"CSM_V3_SinkerRing_V1_0_TESTSLICE.stl\")\n\n# Ensure output directory exists\ntry:\n    os.makedirs(BASE, exist_ok=True)\nexcept:\n    pass\n\n# =============================================================================\n# DIMENSIONS (all in mm)\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Ring main body\nRING_OD = 135.0\nRING_ID = 114.8           # 0.5mm total clearance over 114.3mm cylinder\nRING_HEIGHT = 8.0\nRING_OUTER_R = RING_OD / 2  # 67.5mm\nRING_INNER_R = RING_ID / 2  # 57.4mm\n\n# Sinker dimensions\nNUM_SINKERS = 72\nSINKER_PITCH_DEG = 360.0 / NUM_SINKERS  # 5.0deg\nSINKER_PHASE_OFFSET_DEG = 2.5   # Offset from needle slot (between needles)\n\nSINKER_WIDTH = 1.2        # mm circumferential thickness\nSINKER_RADIAL_EXT = 3.0   # mm extension inward from RING_INNER_R\nSINKER_HEIGHT_FULL = 4.0  # mm axial height (full sinkers)\nSINKER_HEIGHT_MIN = 2.0   # mm axial height at feeder window center\nSINKER_Z_BASE = 2.0       # Z position where sinkers start (base of ring)\n\n# Sinker tip radius (where the holding edge sits)\nSINKER_TIP_R = RING_INNER_R - SINKER_RADIAL_EXT  # 54.4mm\n\n# Feeder windows\nNUM_FEEDERS = 6\nFEEDER_ANGLES = [0, 60, 120, 180, 240, 300]   # 60deg intervals\nFEEDER_WINDOW_ARC_MM = 8.0   # mm circumferential at sinker tip radius\nFEEDER_TAPER_RANGE_DEG = 5.0  # taper extends +/- 5deg from feeder center\n\n# Calculate angular width of feeder window\n# arc_length = radius * angle_rad\nFEEDER_WINDOW_ANGLE_DEG = math.degrees(FEEDER_WINDOW_ARC_MM / RING_INNER_R)\n# About 8deg\n\n# Mounting pins\nPCD_PINS = 125.0           # Pitch circle diameter for dowel pins\nPIN_RADIUS = PCD_PINS / 2  # 62.5mm\nPIN_HOLE_DIA = 4.2         # Slightly larger than 4mm pin (4.0+0.2 clearance)\nPIN_HOLE_R = PIN_HOLE_DIA / 2\nPIN_HOLE_DEPTH = RING_HEIGHT  # Through hole\n\n# Pin positions (3 pins, 1 round + 2 slotted)\n# Place at 30deg, 150deg, 270deg (between feeders)\nPIN_ANGLES = [30, 150, 270]\nPIN_SLOT_LENGTH = 0.6   # mm - slot extends 0.3mm each side for thermal expansion\n\n# Test slice angles\nTEST_SLICE_START = -15.0  # deg (covers feeder at 0deg)\nTEST_SLICE_END = 15.0\n\n# Resolution\nANGULAR_SEGMENTS = 360  # 1deg segments for smooth ring\n\n# =============================================================================\n# UTILITY FUNCTIONS\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef smoothstep(x):\n    \"\"\"Cubic smoothstep easing: f(x) = 3x^2 - 2x^3\n    f(0)=0, f(1)=1, f'(0)=0, f'(1)=0\n    Used for taper transitions to eliminate jerk.\"\"\"\n    if x <= 0:\n        return 0.0\n    if x >= 1:\n        return 1.0\n    return 3 * x * x - 2 * x * x * x\n\n\ndef get_sinker_height(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"Returns sinker height at given angle.\n    \n    Outside feeder zones: SINKER_HEIGHT_FULL (4mm)\n    At feeder centers: SINKER_HEIGHT_MIN (2mm)\n    Smoothstep taper between.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Find closest feeder angle\n    min_dist = 360.0\n    for fa in FEEDER_ANGLES:\n        dist = abs(theta_deg - fa)\n        # Handle wraparound (e.g., 358 is close to 0)\n        if dist > 180:\n            dist = 360 - dist\n        if dist < min_dist:\n            min_dist = dist\n    \n    # If outside taper range, full height\n    if min_dist >= FEEDER_TAPER_RANGE_DEG:\n        return SINKER_HEIGHT_FULL\n    \n    # Within taper range - smoothstep from MIN at 0deg to FULL at TAPER_RANGE\n    progress = min_dist / FEEDER_TAPER_RANGE_DEG  # 0 at center, 1 at edge\n    height_diff = SINKER_HEIGHT_FULL - SINKER_HEIGHT_MIN\n    return SINKER_HEIGHT_MIN + height_diff * smoothstep(progress)\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD SINKER RING\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef build_sinker_ring(slice_only=False):\n    \"\"\"Build the sinker ring with all 72 sinker projections.\n    \n    Args:\n        slice_only: if True, build only the test slice (30deg)\n    \n    Returns:\n        FreeCAD Part shape\n    \"\"\"\n    # 1. Create base ring (cylinder with hole)\n    if slice_only:\n        # For test slice, we need a sector\n        slice_angle = TEST_SLICE_END - TEST_SLICE_START  # 30deg\n        # Create sector by intersection\n        full_outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT)\n        full_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, RING_HEIGHT + 0.1,\n                                       FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.05))\n        full_ring = full_outer.cut(full_inner)\n        \n        # Create sector cutter (large pie slice)\n        sector = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R + 10, RING_HEIGHT + 0.2,\n                                   FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.1),\n                                   FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                                   slice_angle)\n        sector.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\n                     FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                     TEST_SLICE_START)\n        \n        ring = full_ring.common(sector)\n    else:\n        # Full ring\n        outer_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT)\n        inner_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, RING_HEIGHT + 0.1,\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.05))\n        ring = outer_cyl.cut(inner_cyl)\n    \n    # 2. Add 72 sinker projections\n    sinkers_added = 0\n    for i in range(NUM_SINKERS):\n        # Sinker angular position (offset from needle slots)\n        theta_deg = i * SINKER_PITCH_DEG + SINKER_PHASE_OFFSET_DEG\n        # Normalize to 0-360\n        theta_deg = theta_deg % 360\n        \n        # Skip if this sinker is outside test slice region\n        if slice_only:\n            if not (TEST_SLICE_START <= theta_deg <= TEST_SLICE_END):\n                # Also check wraparound\n                norm_theta = theta_deg if theta_deg <= 180 else theta_deg - 360\n                if not (TEST_SLICE_START <= norm_theta <= TEST_SLICE_END):\n                    continue\n        \n        # Get sinker height (tapered near feeders)\n        height = get_sinker_height(theta_deg)\n        \n        # Build sinker projection\n        # Sinker is a small box: width (circumferential) x extension (radial) x height (axial)\n        # Position: tip at SINKER_TIP_R, base at RING_INNER_R\n        \n        theta_rad = math.radians(theta_deg)\n        \n        # Half-width angle for sinker (1.2mm wide at tip radius)\n        half_width_angle = math.degrees((SINKER_WIDTH / 2) / SINKER_TIP_R)\n        \n        # Build sinker as a small wedge using two cylinders\n        # Outer cylinder reaches to RING_INNER_R + small overlap\n        # Inner cylinder reaches to SINKER_TIP_R\n        # Wedge angle = 2 * half_width_angle\n        \n        wedge_angle = 2 * half_width_angle\n        \n        # Create wedge cylinder shape\n        sinker_outer = Part.makeCylinder(\n            RING_INNER_R + 0.1,  # slight overlap with ring\n            height,\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, SINKER_Z_BASE),\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n            wedge_angle\n        )\n        sinker_outer.rotate(\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n            theta_deg - half_width_angle\n        )\n        \n        # Subtract inner part (creates ring-segment shape)\n        sinker_inner = Part.makeCylinder(\n            SINKER_TIP_R,\n            height + 0.2,\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, SINKER_Z_BASE - 0.1),\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n            wedge_angle + 0.1\n        )\n        sinker_inner.rotate(\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n            theta_deg - half_width_angle - 0.05\n        )\n        \n        sinker = sinker_outer.cut(sinker_inner)\n        ring = ring.fuse(sinker)\n        sinkers_added += 1\n    \n    # 3. Add mounting pin holes (only for full ring)\n    if not slice_only:\n        for angle_deg in PIN_ANGLES:\n            angle_rad = math.radians(angle_deg)\n            pin_x = PIN_RADIUS * math.cos(angle_rad)\n            pin_y = PIN_RADIUS * math.sin(angle_rad)\n            \n            # Round hole for first pin, slotted for others\n            if angle_deg == PIN_ANGLES[0]:\n                # Round hole\n                pin_hole = Part.makeCylinder(\n                    PIN_HOLE_R,\n                    PIN_HOLE_DEPTH + 0.1,\n                    FreeCAD.Vector(pin_x, pin_y, -0.05)\n                )\n            else:\n                # Slotted hole - elongated tangentially for thermal expansion\n                # Make a stadium shape (rectangle with rounded ends)\n                # For simplicity, use 2 cylinders + a box\n                tangent_angle_rad = angle_rad + math.pi/2\n                offset_x = (PIN_SLOT_LENGTH/2) * math.cos(tangent_angle_rad)\n                offset_y = (PIN_SLOT_LENGTH/2) * math.sin(tangent_angle_rad)\n                \n                # Two end cylinders\n                hole1 = Part.makeCylinder(\n                    PIN_HOLE_R,\n V1.0\\n# =============================================================================\\n# File:    CSM_V3_SinkerRing_V1_0.FCMacro\\n# Date:    May 2026\\n# Target:  C:\\\\3D-Project\\\\01_MECHANICAL\\\\07_KNITTING\\\\freecad_macros\\\\\\n#\\n# DESIGN INTENT:\\n#   Stationary ring around rotating cylinder with 72 sinker projections.\\n#   Sinkers hold yarn loop while needle drops to form stitch.\\n#   6 feeder windows allow yarn entry without removing sinkers (tapered).\\n#\\n# CRITICAL DESIGN RULES:\\n#   - NEVER remove sinkers at feeder zones (only taper height)\\n#   - Sinkers offset by 2.5deg from needle slots (between needles)\\n#   - All 72 sinkers retain function\\n#   - Smoothstep tapers in feeder zones (no sharp transitions)\\n#\\n# DIMENSIONS (LOCKED):\\n#   Ring OD: 135mm\\n#   Ring ID: 114.8mm (0.5mm clearance over 114.3mm cylinder)\\n#   Height: 8mm\\n#   Sinker: 1.2mm wide x 3.0mm radial x 4mm tall (full)\\n#   Sinker height tapered to 2mm at feeder window centers\\n#   72 sinkers at 5deg pitch (360/72)\\n#   6 feeders at 60deg intervals (0, 60, 120, 180, 240, 300deg)\\n#   Window width: ~8mm circumferential at sinker tip radius\\n#\\n# MOUNTING:\\n#   3 dowel pins at PCD 125mm: 1 round + 2 slotted\\n#   Apply Loctite 603 for retention\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\nimport FreeCAD\\nimport Part\\nimport math\\nimport os\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# CONFIGURATION\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\n# Output paths\\nBASE = r\\\"C:\\\\3D-Project\\\\01_MECHANICAL\\\\07_KNITTING\\\\SinkerRing_V1_0\\\"\\nPATH_STL_FULL  = os.path.join(BASE, \\\"CSM_V3_SinkerRing_V1_0_FULL.stl\\\")\\nPATH_STL_SLICE = =============================================================================\\n# DIMENSIONS (all in mm)\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\n# Ring main body\\nRING_OD = 135.0\\nRING_ID = 114.8           # 0.5mm total clearance over 114.3mm cylinder\\nRING_HEIGHT = 8.0\\nRING_OUTER_R = RING_OD / 2  # 67.5mm\\nRING_INNER_R = RING_ID / 2  # 57.4mm\\n\\n# Sinker dimensions\\nNUM_SINKERS = 72\\nSINKER_PITCH_DEG = 360.0 / NUM_SINKERS  # 5.0deg\\nSINKER_PHASE_OFFSET_DEG = 2.5   # Offset from needle slot (between needles)\\n\\nSINKER_WIDTH = 1.2        # mm circumferential thickness\\nSINKER_RADIAL_EXT = 3.0   # mm extension inward from RING_INNER_R\\nSINKER_HEIGHT_FULL = 4.0  # mm axial height (full sinkers)\\nSINKER_HEIGHT_MIN = 2.0   # mm axial height at feeder window center\\nSINKER_Z_BASE = 2.0       # Z position where sinkers start (base of ring)\\n\\n# Sinker tip radius (where the holding edge sits)\\nSINKER_TIP_R = RING_INNER_R - SINKER_RADIAL_EXT  # 54.4mm\\n\\n# Feeder windows\\nNUM_FEEDERS = 6\\nFEEDER_ANGLES = [0, 60, 120, 180, 240, 300]   # 60deg intervals\\nFEEDER_WINDOW_ARC_MM = 8.0   # mm circumferential at sinker tip radius\\nFEEDER_TAPER_RANGE_DEG = 5.0  # taper extends +/- 5deg from feeder center\\n\\n# Calculate angular width of feeder window\\n# arc_length = radius * angle_rad\\nFEEDER_WINDOW_ANGLE_DEG = math.degrees(FEEDER_WINDOW_ARC_MM / RING_INNER_R)\\n# About 8deg\\n\\n# Mounting pins\\nPCD_PINS = 125.0           # Pitch circle diameter for dowel pins\\nPIN_RADIUS = PCD_PINS / 2  # 62.5mm\\nPIN_HOLE_DIA = 4.2         # Slightly larger than 4mm pin (4.0+0.2 clearance)\\nPIN_HOLE_R = PIN_HOLE_DIA / 2\\nPIN_HOLE_DEPTH = RING_HEIGHT  # Through hole\\n\\n# Pin positions (3 pins, 1 round + 2 slotted)\\n# Place at 30deg, 150deg, 270deg (between feeders)\\nPIN_ANGLES = [30, 150, 270]\\nPIN_SLOT_LENGTH = 0.6   # mm - slot extends 0.3mm each side for thermal expansion\\n\\n# Test slice angles\\nTEST_SLICE_START = -15.0  # deg (covers feeder at 0deg)\\nTEST_SLICE_END = 15.0\\n\\n# Resolution\\nANGULAR_SEGMENTS = 360  # 1deg segments for smooth ring\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# UTILITY FUNCTIONS\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\ndef smoothstep(x):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cubic smoothstep easing: f(x) = 3x^2 - 2x^3\\n    f(0)=0, f(1)=1, f'(0)=0, f'(1)=0\\n    Used for taper transitions to eliminate jerk.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if x <= 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    if x >= 1:\\n        return 1.0\\n    return 3 * x * x - 2 * x * x * x\\n\\n\\ndef get_sinker_height(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Returns sinker height at given angle.\\n    \\n    Outside feeder zones: SINKER_HEIGHT_FULL (4mm)\\n    At feeder centers: SINKER_HEIGHT_MIN (2mm)\\n    Smoothstep taper between.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Find closest feeder angle\\n    min_dist = 360.0\\n    for fa in FEEDER_ANGLES:\\n        dist = abs(theta_deg - fa)\\n        # Handle wraparound (e.g., 358 is close to 0)\\n        if dist > 180:\\n            dist = 360 - dist\\n        if dist < min_dist:\\n            min_dist = dist\\n    \\n    # If outside taper range, full height\\n    if min_dist >= FEEDER_TAPER_RANGE_DEG:\\n        return SINKER_HEIGHT_FULL\\n    \\n    # Within taper range - smoothstep from MIN at 0deg to FULL at TAPER_RANGE\\n    progress = min_dist / FEEDER_TAPER_RANGE_DEG  # 0 at center, 1 at edge\\n    height_diff = SINKER_HEIGHT_FULL - SINKER_HEIGHT_MIN\\n    return SINKER_HEIGHT_MIN + height_diff * smoothstep(progress)\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD SINKER RING\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\ndef build_sinker_ring(slice_only=False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build the sinker ring with all 72 sinker projections.\\n    \\n    Args:\\n        slice_only: if True, build only the test slice (30deg)\\n    \\n    Returns:\\n        FreeCAD Part shape\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # 1. Create base ring (cylinder with hole)\\n    if slice_only:\\n        # For test slice, we need a sector\\n        slice_angle = TEST_SLICE_END - TEST_SLICE_START  # 30deg\\n        # Create sector by intersection\\n        full_outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT)\\n        full_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, RING_HEIGHT + 0.1,\\n                                       FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.05))\\n        full_ring = full_outer.cut(full_inner)\\n        \\n        # Create sector cutter (large pie slice)\\n        sector = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R + 10, RING_HEIGHT + 0.2,\\n                                   FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.1),\\n                                   FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                                   slice_angle)\\n        sector.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\\n                     FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                     TEST_SLICE_START)\\n        \\n        ring = full_ring.common(sector)\\n    else:\\n        # Full ring\\n        outer_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT)\\n        inner_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, RING_HEIGHT + 0.1,\\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.05))\\n        ring = outer_cyl.cut(inner_cyl)\\n    \\n    # 2. Add 72 sinker projections\\n    sinkers_added = 0\\n    for i in range(NUM_SINKERS):\\n        # Sinker angular position (offset from needle slots)\\n        theta_deg = i * SINKER_PITCH_DEG + SINKER_PHASE_OFFSET_DEG\\n        # Normalize to 0-360\\n        theta_deg = theta_deg % 360\\n        \\n        # Skip if this sinker is outside test slice region\\n        if slice_only:\\n            if not (TEST_SLICE_START <= theta_deg <= TEST_SLICE_END):\\n                # Also check wraparound\\n                norm_theta = theta_deg if theta_deg <= 180 else theta_deg - 360\\n                if not (TEST_SLICE_START <= norm_theta <= TEST_SLICE_END):\\n                    continue\\n        \\n        # Get sinker height (tapered near feeders)\\n        height = get_sinker_height(theta_deg)\\n        \\n        # Build sinker projection\\n        # Sinker is a small box: width (circumferential) x extension (radial) x height (axial)\\n        # Position: tip at SINKER_TIP_R, base at RING_INNER_R\\n        \\n        theta_rad = math.radians(theta_deg)\\n        \\n        # Half-width angle for sinker (1.2mm wide at tip radius)\\n        half_width_angle = math.degrees((SINKER_WIDTH / 2) / SINKER_TIP_R)\\n        \\n        # Build sinker as a small wedge using two cylinders\\n        # Outer cylinder reaches to RING_INNER_R + small overlap\\n        # Inner cylinder reaches to SINKER_TIP_R\\n        # Wedge angle = 2 * half_width_angle\\n        \\n        wedge_angle = 2 * half_width_angle\\n        \\n        # Create wedge cylinder shape\\n        sinker_outer = Part.makeCylinder(\\n            RING_INNER_R + 0.1,  # slight overlap with ring\\n            height,\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, SINKER_Z_BASE),\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n            wedge_angle\\n        )\\n        sinker_outer.rotate(\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n            theta_deg - half_width_angle\\n        )\\n        \\n        # Subtract inner part (creates ring-segment shape)\\n        sinker_inner = Part.makeCylinder(\\n            SINKER_TIP_R,\\n            height + 0.2,\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, SINKER_Z_BASE - 0.1),\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n            wedge_angle + 0.1\\n        )\\n        sinker_inner.rotate(\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\\n            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n            theta_deg - half_width_angle - 0.05\\n        )\\n        \\n        sinker = sinker_outer.cut(sinker_inner)\\n        ring = ring.fuse(sinker)\\n        sinkers_added += 1\\n    \\n    # 3. Add mounting pin holes (only for full ring)\\n    if not slice_only:\\n        for angle_deg in PIN_ANGLES:\\n            angle_rad = math.radians(angle_deg)\\n            pin_x = PIN_RADIUS * math.cos(angle_rad)\\n            pin_y = PIN_RADIUS * math.sin(angle_rad)\\n            \\n            # Round hole for first pin, slotted for others\\n            if angle_deg == PIN_ANGLES[0]:\\n                # Round hole\\n                pin_hole = Part.makeCylinder(\\n                    PIN_HOLE_R,\\n                    PIN_HOLE_DEPTH + 0.1,\\n                    FreeCAD.Vector(pin_x, pin_y, -0.05)\\n                )\\n            else:\\n                # Slotted hole - elongated tangentially for thermal expansion\\n                # Make a stadium shape (rectangle with rounded ends)\\n                # For simplicity, use 2 cylinders + a box\\n                tangent_angle_rad = angle_rad + math.pi/2\\n                offset_x = (PIN_SLOT_LENGTH/2) * math.cos(tangent_angle_rad)\\n                offset_y = (PIN_SLOT_LENGTH/2) * math.sin(tangent_angle_rad)\\n                \\n                # Two end cylinders\\n                hole1 = Part.makeCylinder(\\n                    PIN_HOLE_R,\\n                    PIN_HOLE_DEPTH + 0.1,\\n                    FreeCAD.Vector(pin_x + offset_x, pin_y + offset_y, -0.05)\\n                )\\n                hole2 = Part.makeCylinder(\\n                    PIN_HOLE_R,\\n                    PIN_HOLE_DEPTH + 0.1,\\n                    FreeCAD.Vector(pin_x - offset_x, pin_y - offset_y, -0.05)\\n                )\\n                pin_hole = hole1.fuse(hole2)\\n            \\n            ring = ring.cut(pin_hole)\\n    \\n    return ring, sinkers_added\\n\\n\\n# Needle reaches bottom, ready for next cycle\\n\\nKEY INSIGHT:\\n  Sinkers are positioned BETWEEN needle slots\\n  (offset by 2.5deg = half pitch)\\n  Needles rise in slots, sinkers project from gaps\\n\\n  When needle drops, the yarn loop is between two sinkers\\n  Sinkers below the loop hold it up\\n  Needle drops through, forming the stitch\\n\\n===========================================================================\\nCHECK: SINKER WIDTH AT TIP RADIUS\\n===========================================================================\\n\\n  Sinker width: 1.2mm\\n  At tip radius 54.4mm: 1.264deg angular\\n  At ring inner radius 57.4mm: 1.2mm = 1.198deg\\n\\n  Sinker pitch: 5.0deg\\n  Sinker width: ~1.26deg\\n  Gap between sinkers: 3.74deg\\n  Gap width at tip radius: 3.55mm\\n\\n  \u2713 Sinkers are narrow (1.2mm) with large gaps (~3.5mm)\\n  \u2713 Needles can rise through gaps without hitting sinkers\\n\\n\",\"stderr\":\"\"}"

It has 72 small projections (\"sinkers\") that\nhold yarn loops while needles drop to form stitches.\n\nFunction in knitting cycle:\n1. Needle rises in cylinder slot (cam ring lifts it)\n2. Yarn feeder places yarn in needle hook\n3. Needle drops - old loop slides off latch\n4. Old loop catches on sinker projection (sinker holds it up)\n5. Needle drops through, forming the stitch\n\n==========================================\nKEY DIMENSIONS\n==========================================\n\nRing main body:\n  Outer diameter:   135mm\n  Inner diameter:   114.8mm (0.5mm clearance over 114.3mm cylinder)\n  Height:           8mm\n  Wall thickness:   ~10.1mm\n\nSinker projections:\n  Count:            72 (one per needle slot)\n  Pitch:            5.0\u00b0 (360/72)\n  Phase offset:     2.5\u00b0 from needle slots (between needles)\n  Width:            1.2mm circumferential\n  Radial extension: 3.0mm inward from ring ID\n  Tip radius:       54.4mm (over open top of cylinder)\n  Height (full):    4mm\n  Height (taper):   2mm at feeder window centers\n  Z position:       4-8mm (upper half of ring)\n\nFeeder windows:\n  Count:            6 (at 0\u00b0, 60\u00b0, 120\u00b0, 180\u00b0, 240\u00b0, 300\u00b0)\n  Window arc:       ~8mm (~8\u00b0)\n  Taper range:      \u00b15\u00b0 from feeder center\n  Easing:           SMOOTHSTEP cubic (jerk-free)\n  Modified sinkers: 12 of 72 (2 per feeder)\n\nMounting:\n  3 dowel pins at PCD 125mm (62.5mm radius)\n  Pin angles: 30\u00b0, 150\u00b0, 270\u00b0 (between feeders)\n  Pattern: 1 round + 2 slotted (thermal compensation)\n  Pin hole: 4.2mm diameter (4.0mm pin + 0.2mm clearance)\n\n==========================================\nVERTICAL POSITIONING (CRITICAL!)\n==========================================\n\nThe sinker ring is NOT around the cylinder body.\nIt sits at the TOP of the cylinder.\n\nVertical stack:\n  Z=0     \u2500 Cylinder base (drive hub)\n  Z=0-75  \u2500 CYLINDER BODY (75mm tall, OD 114.3mm)\n  Z=75    \u2500 Cylinder TOP (open)\n  Z=75    \u2501 SINKER RING base (sits on cylinder top)\n  Z=75-79 \u2500 Lower ring body (4mm, around cylinder edge)\n  Z=79-83 \u2500 SINKER PROJECTIONS (extend inward, over open cylinder top)\n\nWhy this works:\n  - Sinker tips at radius 54.4mm\n  - Cylinder OD radius is 57.15mm\n  - Sinker tips are 2.75mm INSIDE cylinder OD\n  - BUT they're ABOVE Z=79mm, where cylinder body has ENDED\n  - So sinkers project over the OPEN TOP of cylinder\n  - No interference with rotating cylinder body\n\n==========================================\nWHY 72 SINKERS WITH TAPER (NOT REMOVAL)\n==========================================\n\nEarlier engineering review confirmed:\n  - NEVER remove sinkers at feeder zones\n  - All 72 sinkers must remain (structural function)\n  - Only TAPER height in feeder zones (4mm \u2192 2mm)\n\nThe taper allows:\n  - Yarn to enter feeder window without hitting sinker tops\n  - Sinkers still hold loops (just at lower height)\n  - Smooth transitions (smoothstep easing)\n\nWithout sinkers in feeder zones:\n  - Loops would not be held during feeder operation\n  - Stitches would drop or be inconsistent\n  - Knitting would fail at feeder positions\n\n==========================================\nPRINT INSTRUCTIONS\n==========================================\n\nMaterial:       PETG (DRY!)\nLayer height:   0.16mm\nPerimeters:     4\nTop/bottom:     5 layers\nInfill:         30-40% Gyroid\nOrientation:    Lying flat, sinker projections pointing UP\nSupports:       NONE\nOuter wall:     25 mm/s\nInner wall:     50 mm/s\nInfill:         80-100 mm/s\nCooling:        100% after layer 3\n\nTime estimates:\n  Test slice (30\u00b0): ~45 min, ~25g PETG\n  Full ring:        ~3-4 hours, ~140g PETG\n\n==========================================\nTEST PROTOCOL\n==========================================\n\nPhase 1: Print test slice (30\u00b0 sector covering one feeder)\n  - Width: 1.2mm (verify with calipers)\n  - Taper: smooth transition at feeder zone\n  - No obvious print defects\n\nPhase 3: Light finishing\n  - Sand sinker tips lightly with 1000-grit\n  - Wipe with isopropyl alcohol\n  - Apply PTFE lubricant to contact surfaces\n\nPhase 4: Test fit\n  - Sinker ring should sit on top of cylinder\n  - Cylinder should rotate freely (0.25mm/side clearance)\n  - Sinkers should be above cylinder body\n\nPhase 5: If test passes - print full ring\n  - File: CSM_V3_SinkerRing_V1_0_FULL.stl\n  - Time: ~3-4 hours\n\n==========================================\nDESIGN DECISIONS\n==========================================\n\nWhy 1.2mm sinker width?\n  - At 5\u00b0 pitch, 1.2mm sinker leaves 3.5mm gap\n  - Needles can rise through gaps without obstruction\n  - Sinkers structurally robust at this width\n  - Print tolerance: \u00b10.1mm acceptable\n\nWhy 3.0mm radial extension?\n  - Far enough to hold yarn loop reliably\n  - Not so far as to interfere with needle hook\n  - Tip at 54.4mm = 10.4mm from cylinder centerline\n\nWhy 4mm full height / 2mm minimum?\n  - 4mm ensures positive loop retention\n  - 2mm at feeder zones allows yarn entry\n  - 2:1 ratio is standard for CSM design\n\nWhy \u00b15\u00b0 taper range?\n  - Total taper zone: 10\u00b0 per feeder\n  - 2 sinkers per feeder zone are tapered\n  - Smooth smoothstep transition (no jerk)\n  - 12 of 72 sinkers modified (17%)\n\nWhy pin pattern 30\u00b0/150\u00b0/270\u00b0?\n  - 60\u00b0 offset from feeders (not in same angular zones)\n  - 120\u00b0 apart (3-pin pattern, kinematic)\n  - 1 round + 2 slotted = thermal expansion tolerance\n\n==========================================\nINTERFACES WITH OTHER COMPONENTS\n==========================================\n\nBelow (sits on):\n  \u2192 Cylinder V1.1.1 top face (Z=75mm)\n  \nAbove (will be added):\n  \u2192 Retainer Ring V1.0 (sits on top of sinker ring at Z=83mm)\n  \nAround:\n  \u2192 Cassette Base V1.0 (mounting structure)\n  \nInside:\n  \u2192 Cylinder rotates inside (114.3mm OD, 0.25mm/side clearance)\n\n==========================================\nHARDWARE NEEDED\n==========================================\n\nAlready have (from Orders.xlsx):\n  -
